These writ petitions have been listed on a representation by some of the writ
petitioners seeking an urgent listing stating that the claim of the petitioners would be
adversely affected if any appointments are made and that there is a likelihood of the
appointments being made on 05.02.2021.
Accordingly, permission was granted for listing.
None appears for the petitioner when the matter is called.
Mr. D. Saikia, learned senior counsel is present for the respondent Education
Department.
Adjourned.
 JUDGE
Comparing Assistant
